- **bloodhound-enum**: Active Directory information gathering with BloodHound.py. Use when you need to enumerate domain users, groups, computers, sessions, ACLs, and trust relationships, or to analyze domain attack paths. BloodHound.py is the Python collector for BloodHound; it collects AD data over LDAP/DNS/Kerberos and outputs JSON for analysis in the BloodHound GUI. Use this skill for any scenario involving domain penetration, AD enumeration, or attack path analysis ([DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent](https://github.com/DangerousSONG/attack_skills_for_agent/tree/HEAD/extra-skill/bloodhound-enum))

- **bloodhound-methodology**: Use when mapping Active Directory attack paths on an authorized engagement. Covers choosing and running the BloodHound 4.5.x collector (SharpHound 2.x on Windows, bloodhound-ce-python on Linux), importing data, marking principals as owned, and analyzing paths with prebuilt queries and raw Cypher: shortest paths to Domain Admins, Kerberoastable and AS-REP roastable users, delegation abuse, and session based lateral movement.
